\displaystyle 8{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}}{{\mathcal{C}}^{2}})}+8{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}(0)\right\|}^{2}{({(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2})}

Put together, we get

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{d}{dt}E(\gamma_{t})-2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),v(0)-u(0)\bm{}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}}{{\mathcal{C}}^{2}})}+8{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}(0)\right\|}^{2}{({(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+4{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}+4{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}(0)\right\|}^{2}{({\mathcal{C}}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}+{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}}{{\mathcal{C}}^{2}}+1)}

\displaystyle\quad+8{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}(0)\right\|}^{2}{(2{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})})}

Integrating for
t=\in[0,1]
, and noting that
E(\gamma_{0})={\left\|\gamma^{\prime}(0)\right\|}
,

\displaystyle E{(\gamma_{1})}\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8{(2{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})})})}E{(\gamma_{0})}

\displaystyle\qquad+8{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}}{{\mathcal{C}}^{2}}+1)}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),v(0)-u(0)\bm{}

From Lemma
39
, we can upper bound

\displaystyle 8{(2{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+{\mathcal{C}}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})})}\leq

\displaystyle 8r^{4}e^{2r}+8r^{4}e^{2r}+r^{2}e^{r}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4r^{2}e^{4r}

\displaystyle\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}+\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}}{{\mathcal{C}}^{2}}+1\leq

\displaystyle 4e^{2r}

where we use the fact that
r^{2}\leq e^{2r}/6
for all
r\geq 0
.

The conclusion follows by noting that
\mathrm{d}(x,y)=\sqrt{E{(\gamma_{0})}}
and
\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x}(u),\Exp_{y}(v))}\leq\sqrt{E{(\gamma_{1})}}
.

∎

Lemma 29
.

Let
x,y\in M
. Let
\gamma(s):[0,1]\to M
be a minimizing geodesic between
x
and
y
with
\gamma(0)=x
and
\gamma(1)=y
. Let
u\in T_{x}M
and
v\in T_{y}M
. Let
u(s)
and
v(s)
be the parallel transport of
u
and
v
along
\gamma
. Let
u=u_{1}+u_{2}
and
v=v_{1}+v_{2}
be a decomposition such that
v_{2}=\Gamma_{x}^{y}u_{2}
, where the parallel transport is along
\gamma(s)
.

Let us define
u_{1}(s),u_{2}(s),v_{1}(s)
, all mapping from
[0,1]\to T_{\gamma(s)}M
, such that they are the parallel transport of
u_{1},u_{2},v_{1}
along
\gamma(s)
respectively (
u_{1}(0)=u_{1}
,
u_{2}(0)=u_{2}
,
v_{1}(1)=v_{1}
,
u_{2}(1)=v_{2}
)

Then

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x}(u),\Exp_{y}(v))}^{2}-\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),v(0)-u(0)\bm{}+{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}R{(\gamma^{\prime}(s),(1-s)u(s)+sv(s))}(1-s)u(s)+sv(s),\gamma^{\prime}(s)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle\quad+{(2{\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}+18{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}+{(18{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime})}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}

where
{\mathcal{C}}:=\sqrt{L_{R}}{({\left\|u\right\|}+{\left\|v\right\|})}
and
{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}:=L_{R}^{\prime}{({\left\|u\right\|}+{\left\|v\right\|})}^{3}
.

Proof.

The proof is similar to Lemma
28
. Let us consider the length function
E(\gamma)=\int_{0}^{1}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}(s)\right\|}^{2}ds
. We define a variation of geodesics
\Lambda(s,t)
:

\displaystyle\Lambda(s,t):=\Exp_{\gamma(s)}{(t{(u(s)+s(v(s)-u(s)))})}

We verify that

\displaystyle\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)=

\displaystyle\gamma^{\prime}(s)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)=

\displaystyle u(s)+s(v(s)-u(s))

\displaystyle D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)=

\displaystyle v(s)-u(s)

Consider a fixed
t
, and let
\gamma_{t}(s):=\Lambda(s,t)
(so
\gamma_{t}^{\prime}(s)
is the velocity wrt
s
).

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}E(\gamma_{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{0}^{1}{\left\|\gamma_{t}^{\prime}(s)\right\|}^{2}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma_{t}^{\prime}(s),D_{t}\gamma_{t}^{\prime}(s)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\bm{}ds

and

\displaystyle\frac{d^{2}}{dt^{2}}E(\gamma_{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\bm{}ds+\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}^{2}ds-\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}R{(\partial_{s}(\Lambda(s,t)),\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t))}\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t),\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}^{2}ds-\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}R{(\partial_{s}(\Lambda(s,t)),\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0))}\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t),\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}^{2}ds-\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}R{(\partial_{s}(\Lambda(s,0)),\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0))}\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t),\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}4L_{R}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+4L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}ds

where the second equality uses the Jacobi equation.

The Riemannian curvature tensor term can be simplified as

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{1}2\bm{\left\langle}R{(\partial_{s}(\Lambda(s,0)),\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0))}\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t),\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}R{(\gamma^{\prime}(s),(1-s)u(s)+sv(s))}(1-s)u(s)+sv(s),\gamma^{\prime}(s)\bm{}ds

We further bound

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}2{({{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|})}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}\int_{0}^{1}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}ds

\displaystyle\quad+2{{\mathcal{C}}}^{2}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}^{2}\int_{0}^{1}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}ds+4{\mathcal{C}}\int_{0}^{1}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}^{2}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2{{\mathcal{C}}}^{2}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+4{\mathcal{C}}\int_{0}^{1}\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}+2{({\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}+{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}

where we use Lemma
26
and Lemma
39
.

We also bound

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}4L_{R}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4{\mathcal{C}}^{2}{(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}+\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}}{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|u(0)-v(0)\right\|})}{({(\cosh{({\mathcal{C}})}-1)}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}+{(\frac{\sinh{({\mathcal{C}})}}{{\mathcal{C}}}-1)}{\left\|u(0)-v(0)\right\|})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 16{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+16{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|u(0)-v(0)\right\|}^{2}

where we use Lemma
26
and Lemma
39
.

We finally bound

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}4L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}ds\leq 4{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}

by definition of
{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}
.

Combining the above bounds,

\displaystyle E{(\gamma_{1})}=\left.\frac{d}{dt}E{(\gamma_{t})}\right|_{t=0}+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{r}\frac{d^{2}}{dt^{2}}E{(\gamma_{t})}dtdr

\displaystyle\quad\leq\ 2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),v(0)-u(0)\bm{}+{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}R{(\gamma^{\prime}(s),(1-s)u(s)+sv(s))}(1-s)u(s)+sv(s),\gamma^{\prime}(s)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle\quad+2{({\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}+{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}+2{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+16{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+16{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|u(0)-v(0)\right\|}^{2}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),v(0)-u(0)\bm{}+{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}R{(\gamma^{\prime}(s),(1-s)u(s)+sv(s))}(1-s)u(s)+sv(s),\gamma^{\prime}(s)\bm{}ds

\displaystyle\quad+{(2{\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}+18{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}^{2}+{(18{\mathcal{C}}^{4}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime})}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}^{2}+4{\mathcal{C}}^{2}e^{2{\mathcal{C}}}\mathrm{d}{(x,y)}{\left\|v(0)-u(0)\right\|}

Our conclusion follows as
\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x}(u),\Exp_{y}(v))}^{2}\leq E(\gamma_{1})
.
∎

Lemma 30
.

Let
a_{t},b_{t}:t\to\mathbb{R}^{+}
satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}a_{t}=b_{t}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}b_{t}\leq Ca_{t}

with initial conditions
a_{0},b_{0}
, then for all
t
,

\displaystyle a_{t}\leq{a_{0}}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)}+\frac{b_{0}}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}

\displaystyle b_{t}\leq\sqrt{C}{({a_{0}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}+\frac{b_{0}}{\sqrt{C}}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)})}

Proof.

Let
x_{t}:={a_{0}}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)}+\frac{b_{0}}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}
and
y_{t}:=\sqrt{C}{({a_{0}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}+\frac{b_{0}}{\sqrt{C}}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)})}
. We verify that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}x_{t}=\sqrt{C}{({a_{0}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}+\frac{b_{0}}{\sqrt{C}}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)})}=y_{t}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}y_{t}=C{({a_{0}}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)}+\frac{b_{0}}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)})}=Cx_{t}

We further verify the initial conditions. Note that
\sinh(0)=0
and
cosh(0)=1
. Thus

\displaystyle x_{0}=a_{0}

\displaystyle y_{0}=b_{0}

Finally, we verify that
a_{t}\leq x_{t}
and
b_{t}\leq y_{t}
for all
t
:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}x_{t}-a_{t}=y_{t}-b_{t}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}y_{t}-b_{t}\geq C{(x_{t}-a_{t})}

∎

Lemma 31
.

Let
a_{t},b_{t}:t\to\mathbb{R}^{+}
satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}a_{t}=b_{t}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}b_{t}\leq Ca_{t}+Dt+E

with initial conditions
a_{0}=0,b_{0}=0
, then for all
t
,

\displaystyle a_{t}\leq\frac{E}{C}\cosh(\sqrt{C}t)+\frac{D}{C^{3/2}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}-\frac{D}{C}t-\frac{E}{C}

\displaystyle b_{t}\leq\frac{E}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh(\sqrt{C}t)+\frac{D}{C}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)}-\frac{D}{C}

Proof.

Let
x_{t}:=\frac{E}{C}\cosh(\sqrt{C}t)+\frac{D}{C^{3/2}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}-\frac{D}{C}t-\frac{E}{C}
and

y_{t}:=\frac{E}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh(\sqrt{C}t)+\frac{D}{C}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)}-\frac{D}{C}
.

We verify that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}x_{t}=\frac{E}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh(\sqrt{C}t)+\frac{D}{C}\cosh{(\sqrt{C}t)}-\frac{D}{C}=y_{t}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}y_{t}=E\cosh(\sqrt{C}t)+\frac{D}{\sqrt{C}}\sinh{(\sqrt{C}t)}=Cx_{t}+Dt+E

we also verify the initial conditions that
x_{0}=0
and
y_{0}=0
.

∎

Lemma 32
.

Let
x,y\in M
, and let
E_{1}...E_{d}
be an orthonormal basis at
T_{x}M
.
Let
v\in T_{x}M
be a random vector with
\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}E_{i},v\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}E_{j},v\bm{}}\right]={\mathbbm{1}\left\{i=j\right\}}
.
Let
\gamma:[0,1]\to M
be any smooth path between
x
and
y
. Let
v(t)
be the parallel transport of
v
along
\gamma
. Then for any basis
E^{\prime}_{1}...E^{\prime}_{d}
at
T_{y}M
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}v(t),E_{i}^{\prime}\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}v(t),E_{j}^{\prime}\bm{}}\right]={\mathbbm{1}\left\{i=j\right\}}

In other words, if
v
has identity covariance, then the parallel transport of
v
has identity covariance.

Proof.

Let
E_{i}(t)
be an orthonormal frame along
\gamma
with
E_{i}(0)=E_{i}
.
Under parallel transport,
\frac{d}{dt}\bm{\left\langle}v(t),E_{i}(t)\bm{}=0
. Thus for all
t
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}E_{i}(t),v\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}E_{j}(t),v\bm{}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}E_{i}(0),v\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}E_{j}(0),v\bm{}}\right]={\mathbbm{1}\left\{i=j\right\}}

Finally, consider any basis
E_{i}^{\prime}
. Let
E_{i}^{\prime}=\sum_{k}\alpha^{i}_{k}E_{i}(1)
, i.e.
\alpha^{i}_{k}=\bm{\left\langle}E_{i}^{\prime},E_{k}(1)\bm{}
Then

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}v,E_{i}^{\prime}\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}v,E_{j}^{\prime}\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k,l}\alpha^{i}_{j}\alpha^{j}_{l}\bm{\left\langle}v,E_{k}\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}v,E_{l}\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k,l}\alpha^{i}_{j}\alpha^{j}_{l}{\mathbbm{1}\left\{k=l\right\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\alpha^{i}_{k}\alpha^{j}_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}E_{i}^{\prime},E_{j}^{\prime}\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{i=j\right\}}

∎

Lemma 33
.

Let
x,y\in M
, and let
E_{1}...E_{d}
be an orthonormal basis at
T_{x}M
.

Let
\alpha
denote a spherically symmetric random variable in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, i.e. for any orthogonal matrix
G\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}

\displaystyle\alpha\overset{d}{=}G\alpha

Then for any
x\in M
, let
E_{1}...E_{d}
and
E_{1}^{\prime}...E_{d}^{\prime}
be two sets of orthonormal bases of
T_{x}M
. then

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E_{i}\overset{d}{=}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E_{i}^{\prime}

Consequently, let
v\in T_{x}M:=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E_{i}
. Let
\gamma:[0,1]\to M
be any differentiable path between
x
and
y
. Let
v(t)
be the parallel transport of
v
along
\gamma
. Then for any orthogonal basis
E^{\prime}_{1}...E^{\prime}_{d}
at
T_{y}M
,

\displaystyle v(1)\overset{d}{=}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E_{i}^{\prime}

Proof.

First, we verify that if
\alpha
is spherically symmetric, and
E_{1}..E_{d}
,
E^{\prime}_{1}...E^{\prime}_{d}
are two sets of orthonormal basis at some point
x
, then

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E_{i}\overset{d}{=}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E^{\prime}_{i}

To see this, notice that there exists an orthogonal matrix
G
, with
G_{i,j}=\bm{\left\langle}E_{i},E^{\prime}_{j}\bm{}
, such that

\displaystyle E_{i}=\sum_{j=1}^{d}G_{j,i}E^{\prime}_{j}

We further verify that
G_{i,j}
is orthogonal. It suffices to verify that
GG^{T}=I
.

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{j=k\right\}}=\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{i},E^{\prime}_{j}\bm{}=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{i},E_{k}\bm{}E_{j},\sum_{\ell=1}^{d}\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{j},E_{\ell}\bm{}E_{\ell}\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k,\ell}\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{i},E_{k}\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{j},E_{\ell}\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}E_{j},E_{\ell}\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k}\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{i},E_{k}\bm{}\bm{\left\langle}E^{\prime}_{j},E_{k}\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}G_{i,\cdot},G_{j,\cdot}\bm{}

Note that the inner product on the last line is dot product over
\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and the inner product on preceding lines are over
T_{x}M
. The above implies that

\displaystyle GG^{T}=I

i.e.
G
is orthogonal.

Now consider any arbitrary function
f:T_{x}M\to\mathbb{R}
, then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{f{(\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}E_{i})}}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{f{(\sum_{i}\sum_{j}\alpha_{i}G_{i,j}E^{\prime}_{j})}}\right]

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{f{(\sum_{j}\beta_{j}E^{\prime}_{j})}}\right]

where we defined
\beta_{j}:=\sum_{i}\alpha_{i}G_{i,j}
. We finally verify that
\beta\overset{d}{=}\alpha
. This follows from the fact that
\beta=G^{T}\alpha
, where
G
is an orthogonal matrix, and the definition of spherical symmetry for
\alpha
.

Consider an arbitrary line
\gamma(t):[0,1]\to M
, with
x:=\gamma(0)
,
y:=\gamma(1)
. Let
E_{i}
be an orthonormal basis at
T_{x}M
, and
E_{i}(t)
be an orthonormal basis at
T_{\gamma(t)}M
obtained from parallel transport of
E_{i}
. This proves the first claim.

To verify the second claim, let
v\in T_{x}M
be a random vector, given by

\displaystyle v=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\alpha_{i}E_{i}

where
\alpha
is some spherically random vector in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Let
v(t)
be the parallel transport of
v
along
\gamma
. Let
\alpha(t):=\bm{\left\langle}v(t),E_{i}(t)\bm{}
. Then by definition of parallel transport, for all
i
,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\bm{\left\langle}v(t),E_{i}(t)\bm{}=0

so that for all
t\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle\alpha(t):=\alpha

the second claim then follows from the first claim.

∎

Appendix E
Matrix ODE

In this section we provide Gronwall-style inequality for matrix ODE. The results in this section are necessary for analyzing Jacobi Equation, whose coordinates with respect to some orthonormal frame can be viewed as an ODE in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. In particular, Lemma
28
and Lemma
29
rely on results in this section.

Lemma 34

(Formal Matrix Exponent)
.

Given
\mathbf{M}(t):\mathbb{R}^{+}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, define
\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}:\mathbb{R}^{+}\to\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
as the solution to the matrix ODE

\displaystyle\emat{(0;\mathbf{M})}=

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}(t)\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}

Then

1.

Let
\mathbf{x}(t)
be the solution to the ODE
\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{x}(t)=\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{x}(t)
, for some
\mathbf{M}
, then

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}(t)=\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{x}(0)

2.

Let
\mathbf{z}(t)
be the solution to
\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{z}(t)=\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{z}(t)+\mathbf{v}(t)
, for some
\mathbf{M}
,
\mathbf{v}
, then

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}(T)=\int_{0}^{T}\emat{(T-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})}\mathbf{v}(s)ds+\emat{(T;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{z}(0)

where for any
s,t
,
\mathbf{N}_{s}(t):=\mathbf{M}(s+t)
.

Proof of Lemma
34
.

Let
\mathbf{y}_{t}:=\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{x}(0)
. We verify that

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(0)=

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{y}(t)=

\displaystyle{(\frac{d}{dt}\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})})}\mathbf{x}(0)=\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{y}(t)

Given the same dynamics and initial conditions, we conclude that
\mathbf{x}(t)=\mathbf{y}(t)
for all
t
.

To verify the second claim, note that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{0}^{t}\emat{(t-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})}\mathbf{v}(s)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\emat{(0;\mathbf{N}_{t})}\mathbf{v}(s)+\int_{0}^{t}{(\frac{d}{dt}\emat{(t-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})})}\mathbf{v}(s)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(s)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{N}_{s}(t-s)\emat{(t-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})}\mathbf{v}(s)dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(s)+\mathbf{M}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\emat{(t-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})}\mathbf{v}(s)ds

Additionally,
\frac{d}{dt}\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{z}(0)=\mathbf{M}(t)\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{z}(0)
, summing,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{z}(t)=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{0}^{t}\emat{(t-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})}\mathbf{v}(s)ds+\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{z}(0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(s)+\mathbf{M}(t)\int_{0}^{t}\emat{(t-s;\mathbf{N}_{s})}\mathbf{v}(s)ds+\mathbf{M}(t)\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\mathbf{z}(0)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}(s)+\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{z}(t)

∎

Lemma 35
.

Let
\emat
be as defined in Lemma
34
. Let

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}:=\emat{(t;\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(t)&0\end{bmatrix})}

for some
\mathbf{M}(t)
. Assume
{\left\|\mathbf{M}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{M}}
for all
t
. Then for all
t
,

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

and

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)-1

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)-tI\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-t

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-1

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}e^{L_{\mathbf{M}}}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)-tI\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{6}L_{\mathbf{M}}e^{L{\mathbf{M}}}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}e^{L_{\mathbf{M}}}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle L_{\mathbf{M}}e^{L_{\mathbf{M}}}

Proof of Lemma
35
.

We first verify the first part of the lemma. Consider the ODE given by

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}(t)=\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(t)&0\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)\end{bmatrix}

By Lemma
34
,
\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}
satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}

By Cauchy Schwarz,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)\right\|}_{2}

We apply Lemma
30
, with
a_{t}:={\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)\right\|}_{2}
and
b_{t}:={\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)\right\|}_{2}
,
C:=L_{\mathbf{M}}
. Then

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}\right\|}_{2}\leq{{\left\|\mathbf{x}_{0}\right\|}_{2}}\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}+\frac{{\left\|\mathbf{y}_{0}\right\|}_{2}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{y}_{t}\right\|}_{2}\leq\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}{({{\left\|\mathbf{x}_{0}\right\|}_{2}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}+\frac{{\left\|\mathbf{y}_{0}\right\|}_{2}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)})}

This immediately implies that

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

This proves the first claim of the Lemma.

We now prove the second claim. We verify that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{x}(t)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{x}(t)\end{bmatrix}=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{x}(t)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t){(\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0))}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\mathbf{M}(t){(\mathbf{x}(0)+t\mathbf{y}(0))}\end{bmatrix}

Thus

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}+L_{\mathbf{M}}{({\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}+t{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2})}

We verify that

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{M}}\int_{0}^{t}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(s)-s\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}ds+\frac{t^{2}}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}

Let us apply Lemma
31
with
a_{t}={\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}
,
b_{t}={\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}
,
C=L_{\mathbf{M}}
,
D=L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}
and
E=L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}}\cosh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)+\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}^{3/2}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t-\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}{(\cosh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)-1)}+{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}{(\frac{1}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)+\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}}\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-\frac{L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}\sinh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)+{\left\|\mathbf{y}(0)\right\|}_{2}{(\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-1)}

(E.1)

Again from Lemma
34
, we know that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}

thus

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-t\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)-I&\mathbf{B}(t)-tI\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)-I\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}

combined with (
E.1
), and using the fact that the above hold for all
\mathbf{y}(0)
and
\mathbf{x}(0)
, we can bound

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)-1

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)-tI\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-t

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)}-1

Finally, to prove the third claim,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)-t\mathbf{y}(0)-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)-\mathbf{y}(0)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(t)&0\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(0)&0\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{y}(0)\\
\mathbf{v}\end{bmatrix}

∎

Lemma 36
.

Let
\emat
be as defined in Lemma
34
. Let

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}:=\emat{(t;\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(t)&0\end{bmatrix})}

for some
\mathbf{M}(t)
. Assume
{\left\|\mathbf{M}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{M}}
for all
t
. Then for all
t
,

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{{(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)

Proof.

The proof is similar to Lemma
35
. Consider the ODE given by

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}\\
\mathbf{y}\end{bmatrix}(t)=\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(t)&0\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)\end{bmatrix}

with initial condition
\mathbf{y}(0)=0
.

By Lemma
34
,
\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}
satisfies

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(0)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}y(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}y(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\\
\mathbf{M}(t){(\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(0))}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
{(\mathbf{M}(t)-\mathbf{M}(0))}\mathbf{x}(0)\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\end{bmatrix}

By Cauchy Schwarz, for all
t\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}+{(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}

Apply Lemma
31
with
a_{t}={\left\|\mathbf{x}(t)-\mathbf{x}(0)-\frac{t^{2}}{2}\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}
,
b_{t}={\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}
,
C=L_{\mathbf{M}}
,
D=0
and
E={(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}
to get

\displaystyle a_{t}\leq\frac{{(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{L_{\mathbf{M}}}{(\cosh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)-1)}

\displaystyle b_{t}\leq\frac{{(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)

Finally, recall that

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)={(\mathbf{C}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0))}\mathbf{x}(0)

Since we have shown that
{\left\|\mathbf{y}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{{(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}{\left\|\mathbf{x}(0)\right\|}_{2}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)
for all
\mathbf{x}(0)
, it follows that

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{{(L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{2})}}{\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}}\sinh(\sqrt{L_{\mathbf{M}}}t)

∎

Appendix F
Miscellaneous Lemmas

Lemma 37
.

Let
c\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
be such that
c\geq 3
. For any
x
satisfying
x\geq 3c\log c
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{x}{\log x}\geq c

The following Lemma is taken from
Sun et al. 2019
:

Lemma 38
.

For any
x\in M
,
a,y\in T_{x}M

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x}(y+a),\Exp_{\Exp_{x}(a)}{(\Gamma_{x}^{\Exp_{x}(a)}y)})}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}{\left\|a\right\|}{\left\|y\right\|}{({\left\|a\right\|}+{\left\|y\right\|})}e^{\sqrt{L_{R}}{({\left\|a\right\|}+{\left\|y\right\|})}}

Proof.

From the proof of Lemma 3 from
Sun et al. 2019
(which is in turn a refinement of the proof from
Karcher 1977
)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x}(y+a),\Exp_{\Exp_{x}(a)}{(\Gamma_{x}^{\Exp_{x}(a)}y)})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}\frac{\cosh(\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|y+(1-t)a\right\|})-\frac{\sinh(\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|y+(1-t)a\right\|})}{\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|y+(1-t)a\right\|}}}{{\left\|y+(1-t)a\right\|}}dt\cdot{\left\|a\right\|}{\left\|y\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{L_{R}}\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|y+(1-t)a\right\|}e^{\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|y+(1-t)a\right\|}}dt\cdot{\left\|a\right\|}{\left\|y\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}{\left\|a\right\|}{\left\|y\right\|}{({\left\|a\right\|}+{\left\|y\right\|})}e^{\sqrt{L_{R}}{({\left\|a\right\|}+{\left\|y\right\|})}}

where we use the fact from Lemma
39
that for all
r\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\frac{\cosh(r)}{r}-\frac{\sinh(r)}{r^{2}}\leq re^{r}

∎

Lemma 39
.

For all
r\geq 0
,

\displaystyle\sinh(r)\leq re^{r}

\displaystyle\cosh(r)-1\leq\frac{r^{2}}{2}e^{r}

\displaystyle\frac{\cosh(r)}{r}-\frac{\sinh(r)}{r^{2}}\leq re^{r}

\displaystyle\cosh(r)\leq e^{r}

\displaystyle\frac{\sinh(r)}{r}-1\leq r^{2}e^{3}

Proof.

Elementary computation from power series.
∎
